**任务**：两个玩家视觉系统 1:1 补齐。文件权属：src/entities/Player.ts（只许动死亡/眼态相关段与文件尾部新方法，**严禁触碰 Run/跳跃/水中 liqFactor 段**——刚由并行修复收敛）、src/render/Renderer.ts（drawPlayer 段 + 新叠层；严禁触碰 TownNPC 渲染与 Game 逻辑）、src/player/PaperDoll.ts（加 part 过滤）。禁改 Game.ts/TownNPC.ts/Buffs.ts。

**①死亡表现**（审计#1，现在 drawPlayer 开头 `if (p.dead) return` 整段缺失是错的）：
- 原版：初速 Player.cs:38287-38293（头/身/腿三段 Y=Next(-40,-10)*0.1、X=Next(-20,21)*0.1+2*hitDirection；stoned 时三段归零 :38294-38298）；UpdateDead :16878-16888（每 tick 三段 pos+=vel、rot+=vel.X*0.1、vel.Y+=0.1、vel.X*=0.99 翻滚解体；immuneAlpha+=2 至 255 → GetImmuneAlpha(:53253)=(255-alpha)/255 渐隐 ≈2.1s）；绘制不跳过（LegacyPlayerRenderer.cs:479-482）；中核难度 respawnTimer 归零后画幽灵（Player.cs:16902 + ghostFrame 每 8t 前进 %4 :6435-6450，Ghost.png 4 帧贴图查 terraria-assets 与 public/sprites/vanilla/Ghost.png）。
- 我们：死亡入口在 Player.damage/die 链（自寻 dead 置位点与 respawn 计时——respawnTimer 在 Game 侧，只读不改）；渲染需把纸娃娃拆 head/body/legs 三件分别画（PaperDoll 加 part 过滤通道：'head'|'body'|'legs' 各自合成——复用现有 compositePaperDoll 管线，把 VERTICAL_CHANNELS 只留对应件），三件各带 pos/rot/vel 散飞+统一渐隐 alpha。石化解体归零门照抄。备不齐三件拆分时的兜底：整体单帧带 fullRotation 翻滚（登记偏离）——但先做真拆分。
- 幽灵：难度==中核(mediumcore?查我们难度枚举——Main.difficulty==2 语义)且 respawn 到点 → Ghost.png 帧动画画在死亡位。

**②眨眼/眼态**（审计#5）：
- 原版 PlayerEyeHelper.cs 全文状态机：正常每 240t 眨一次（半闭 2t→闭 2t→半闭 2t）；ZoneSandstorm/雪原雨=眯眼(squint) 120t 周期；中度受伤(hp<0.75max?查源码阈值)/醉酒 Tipsy/中毒 Poisoned=耷拉眼(droopy)；床中 60/120t 闭合；blind(黑暗 debuff 33)常闭；受击 20t 全闭；EyeFrameToShow 输出 0-2。渲染 PlayerDrawLayers DrawPlayer_21_Head_TheFace_Eyelid：Players[skinVariant,15] 三帧按 EyeFrameToShow 叠画在头/眼之上（带 OffsetsPlayerHeadgear 行偏移）。素材 Player_0_15.png 已在库。
- 我们：PaperDoll VERTICAL_CHANNELS 恒睁眼；眨眼状态机加 Player（eyeState 字段+tick），渲染在纸娃娃头层之上叠 eyelid 帧（PaperDoll 通道 15）。

修完验证：`npx tsc --noEmit` 三文件零错；探针 _deathvis.mjs：清空玩家血量致死（p.hp=0 经 damage 或直接触发 die 链——查现有死亡入口；也可 p.damage(99999)）→ 断言死亡后 g.player.dead===true 且渲染画布在死亡位仍有非空像素采样（三件散飞）、2.2s 后渐隐；眨眼探针：采样 eyeState 在 240t 周期内出现非 0 值。死亡会导致 respawn 界面——探针在死亡后立即采样再关闭即可。vitest 跑 tests/player-run-parity.test.ts 确认没动 Run 段（10/10 必须保持）。回报：改动行号+原版行号对照+探针数字+若三件拆分失败说明兜底偏离。用中文。

51				eyeFrameToShow = ((_timeInState % 120 - 114 < 0) ? EyeFrame.EyeHalfClosed : EyeFrame.EyeClosed);
52				break;
53			case EyeState.IsModeratelyDamaged:
54			case EyeState.IsTipsy:
55			case EyeState.IsPoisoned:
56				eyeFrameToShow = ((_timeInState % 120 - 100 < 0) ? EyeFrame.EyeHalfClosed : EyeFrame.EyeClosed);
57				break;
58			case EyeState.InBed:
59			{
60				EyeFrame eyeFrame = (DoesPlayerCountAsModeratelyDamaged(player) ? EyeFrame.EyeHalfClosed : EyeFrame.EyeOpen);
61				_timeInState = player.sleeping.timeSleeping;
62				eyeFrameToShow = ((_timeInState >= 60) ? ((_timeInState < 120) ? EyeFrame.EyeHalfClosed : EyeFrame.EyeClosed) : eyeFrame);
63				break;
64			}
65			case EyeState.IsBlind:
66				eyeFrameToShow = EyeFrame.EyeClosed;
67				break;
68			case EyeState.JustTookDamage:
69				eyeFrameToShow = EyeFrame.EyeClosed;
70				break;
71			}
72			EyeFrameToShow = (int)eyeFrameToShow;
73		}
74	
75		private void SetStateByPlayerInfo(Player player)
76		{
77			if (player.blackout || player.blind)
78			{
79				SwitchToState(EyeState.IsBlind);
80			}
81			else
82			{
83				if (_state == EyeState.JustTookDamage && _timeInState < 20)
84				{
85					return;
86				}
87				if (player.sleeping.isSleeping)
88				{
89					bool resetStateTimerEvenIfAlreadyInState = player.itemAnimation > 0;
90					SwitchToState(EyeState.InBed, resetStateTimerEvenIfAlreadyInState);
91					return;
92				}
93				if (DoesPlayerCountAsModeratelyDamaged(player))
94				{
95					SwitchToState(EyeState.IsModeratelyDamaged);
96					return;
97				}
98				if (player.tipsy)
99				{
100					SwitchToState(EyeState.IsTipsy);
101					return;
102				}
103				if (player.poisoned || player.venom || player.starving)
104				{
105					SwitchToState(EyeState.IsPoisoned);
106					return;
107				}
108				bool flag = player.ZoneSandstorm || (player.ZoneSnow && Main.IsItRaining);
109				if (player.behindBackWall)
110				{
111					flag = false;
112				}
113				if (flag)
114				{
115					SwitchToState(EyeState.InStorm);
116				}
117				else
118				{
119					SwitchToState(EyeState.NormalBlinking);
120				}
121			}
122		}
123	
124		private void SwitchToState(EyeState newState, bool resetStateTimerEvenIfAlreadyInState = false)
125		{
126			if (_state != newState || resetStateTimerEvenIfAlreadyInState)
127			{
128				_state = newState;
129				_timeInState = 0;
130			}
131		}
132	
133		private bool DoesPlayerCountAsModeratelyDamaged(Player player)
134		{
135			return (float)player.statLife <= (float)player.statLifeMax2 * 0.25f;
136		}
137	
138		public void BlinkBecausePlayerGotHurt()
139		{
140			SwitchToState(EyeState.JustTookDamage, resetStateTimerEvenIfAlreadyInState: true);
141		}
142	}
143

	{

			camera.SpriteBatch.Draw(TextureAssets.Extra[37].Value, new Vector2((int)(position.X - camera.UnscaledPosition.X - (float)(drawPlayer.bodyFrame.Width / 2) + (float)(drawPlayer.width / 2)), (int)(position.Y - camera.UnscaledPosition.Y + (float)drawPlayer.height - (float)drawPlayer.bodyFrame.Height + 8f)) + drawPlayer.bodyPosition + new Vector2(drawPlayer.bodyFrame.Width / 2, drawPlayer.bodyFrame.Height / 2), null, Lighting.GetColor((int)((double)position.X + (double)drawPlayer.width * 0.5) / 16, (int)((double)position.Y + (double)drawPlayer.height * 0.5) / 16, Color.White), 0f, new Vector2(TextureAssets.Extra[37].Width() / 2, TextureAssets.Extra[37].Height() / 2), 1f, spriteEffects, 0f);
		}
	}

	private void DrawGhost(Camera camera, Player drawPlayer, Vector2 position, float shadow = 0f)
	{
		byte mouseTextColor = Main.mouseTextColor;
		SpriteEffects effects = ((drawPlayer.direction != 1) ? SpriteEffects.FlipHorizontally : SpriteEffects.None);
		Color immuneAlpha = drawPlayer.GetImmuneAlpha(Lighting.GetColor((int)((double)drawPlayer.position.X + (double)drawPlayer.width * 0.5) / 16, (int)((double)drawPlayer.position.Y + (double)drawPlayer.height * 0.5) / 16, new Color(mouseTextColor / 2 + 100, mouseTextColor / 2 + 100, mouseTextColor / 2 + 100, mouseTextColor / 2 + 100)), shadow);
		immuneAlpha.A = (byte)((float)(int)immuneAlpha.A * (1f - Math.Max(0.5f, shadow - 0.5f)));
		Rectangle value = new Rectangle(0, TextureAssets.Ghost.Height() / 4 * drawPlayer.ghostFrame, TextureAssets.Ghost.Width(), TextureAssets.Ghost.Height() / 4);
		Vector2 origin = new Vector2((float)value.Width * 0.5f, (float)value.Height * 0.5f);
		camera.SpriteBatch.Draw(TextureAssets.Ghost.Value, new Vector2((int)(position.X - camera.UnscaledPosition.X + (float)(value.Width / 2)), (int)(position.Y - camera.UnscaledPosition.Y + (float)(value.Height / 2))), value, immuneAlpha, 0f, origin, 1f, effects, 0f);
	}
}

228	    const img = sheetRect(a.skinVariant, ch.sheet);

338	export function dollFrame(doll: HTMLCanvasElement, frame: number): { img: HTMLCanvasElement; sx: number; sy: number; sw: number; sh: number } {
339	  const idx = Math.max(0, Math.min(BODY_FRAMES - 1, frame));

343	/** 行走动画帧号（UICharacter.UpdateAnim：(time/0.07 % 14 + 6)） */

351	/** 逐通道 RGB 缩调（stealth 分层消费：armor ×s'/B×settled、星璇 Lerp 通道）。
352	 *  multiply 铺 rgb(255·m) 逐通道乘 + destination-in 保原 alpha；mul 量化 1/128 防缓存爆 */
353	export function tintRGBA(src: HTMLCanvasElement, mul: [number, number, number]): HTMLCanvasElement {
354	  const q = mul.map((m) => Math.round(m * 128) / 128);
355	  const key = `${q[0]},${q[1]},${q[2]}`;
356	  let inner = stealthTintCache.get(src);
357	  if (!inner) { inner = new Map(); stealthTintCache.set(src, inner); }
358	  const hit = inner.get(key);
359	  if (hit) return hit;
360	  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
361	  c.width = src.width; c.height = src.height;
362	  const ctx = c.getContext('2d')!;
363	  ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled = false;
364	  ctx.drawImage(src, 0, 0);
365	  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'multiply';
366	  ctx.fillStyle = `rgb(${Math.round(q[0] * 255)},${Math.round(q[1] * 255)},${Math.round(q[2] * 255)})`;
367	  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
368	  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-in';
369	  ctx.drawImage(src, 0, 0);
370	  ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
371	  inner.set(key, c);
372	  if (inner.size > 48) {   // 量化档 ≤129；stealth 连续变化时留中段档位
373	    let i = 0;
374	    for (const k of inner.keys()) { if (i++ > 24) break; inner.delete(k); }
375	  }
376	  return c;
377	}

58	export function defaultAppearance(name = ''): Appearance {
59	  return {
60	    name,
61	    hair: 0,               // 0 基（贴图 Player_Hair_1.png）
62	    skinVariant: 0,
63	    ...structuredClone(DEFAULT_COLORS),
64	    difficulty: 0,
65	    team: 0,
66	  };
67	}
68	
69	/** 旧数据/损坏数据补默认；颜色钳到 0-255 */
70	export function validateAppearance(a: Partial<Appearance> | null | undefined): Appearance {
71	  const d = defaultAppearance();
72	  if (!a) return d;
73	  const c = (v: unknown, fallback: RGBColor): RGBColor => {
74	    if (typeof v !== 'object' || v === null) return fallback;
75	    const o = v as Partial<RGBColor>;
76	    const cl = (n: unknown) => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(Number(n) || 0)));
77	    return { r: cl(o.r), g: cl(o.g), b: cl(o.b) };
78	  };
79	  return {
80	    name: typeof a.name === 'string' ? a.name.slice(0, 20) : d.name,
81	    // 1.4.5.6 保护（Player.cs:54209）：hair >= 228 → 0
82	    hair: clampInt(a.hair, 0, HAIR_COUNT - 1, d.hair),
83	    skinVariant: clampInt(a.skinVariant, 0, 11, d.skinVariant),
84	    hairColor: c(a.hairColor, d.hairColor),
85	    skinColor: c(a.skinColor, d.skinColor),
86	    eyeColor: c(a.eyeColor, d.eyeColor),
87	    shirtColor: c(a.shirtColor, d.shirtColor),
88	    undershirtColor: c(a.undershirtColor, d.undershirtColor),
89	    pantsColor: c(a.pantsColor, d.pantsColor),
90	    shoeColor: c(a.shoeColor, d.shoeColor),
91	    difficulty: ([0, 1, 2, 3] as number[]).includes(a.difficulty as number) ? (a.difficulty as Difficulty) : 0,
92	  team: (typeof a.team === 'number' && a.team >= 0 && a.team <= 5) ? Math.trunc(a.team) : 0,
93	    hardcoreDead: a.hardcoreDead === true ? true : undefined,   // 硬核消亡标记（旧数据缺省未消亡）
94	  };
95	}
96

/** 47/49 锚点按身体帧行修正（anchor[1] = hy − 22） */
export function wingAnchorForRow(d: WingDraw, row: number): [number, number] {
  if (!d.headgearRow) return d.anchor;
  return [d.anchor[0], (HEADGEAR_ZERO_ROWS.has(row) ? 0 : 2) - 22];
}

```ts
private updateEyeState(game: GameHooks): void {
  // SetStateByPlayerInfo（:75-122）
  const B = BuffType;
  const blackout = this.buffs.has(B.Blackout), blind = this.buffs.has(B.Darkness);
  if (blackout || blind) {
    this.switchEyeState(EYE_BLIND);
  } else {
    if (this.eyeState === EYE_HURT && this.eyeTimeInState < 20) { /* latch :83-86 */ }
    else if (this.sleeping) {
      this.switchEyeState(EYE_BED, this.itemAnimTicks > 0);   // :87-91 resetStateTimerEvenIfAlreadyInState = itemAnimation>0
    } else if (this.hp <= this.maxHp * 0.25) {
      this.switchEyeState(EYE_HURTDMG);                        // :93-96 DoesPlayerCountAsModeratelyDamaged :133-136
    } else if (this.buffs.has(B.Tipsy)) {
      this.switchEyeState(EYE_TIPSY);
    } else if (this.buffs.has(B.Poisoned) || this.buffs.has(B.Venom)) {
      this.switchEyeState(EYE_POISON);                         // starving 本仓无该 buff——缺省跳过
    } else {
      let flag = !!game.scene?.zoneSandstorm
        || (!!game.scene?.zoneSnow && !!game.world.weather.raining);   // :108
      // behindBackWall（玩家自置墙后免眯眼）本仓无追踪——恒 false（偏离登记）
      this.switchEyeState(flag ? EYE_STORM : EYE_BLINK);
    }
  }
  // UpdateEyeFrameToShow（:39-73）
  let frame = 0;
  const t = this.eyeTimeInState;
  switch (this.eyeState) {
    case EYE_BLINK: {
      const n = t % 240 - 234;
      frame = n >= 4 ? 1 : n < 2 ? (n >= 0 ? 1 : 0) : 2;
      break;
    }
    case EYE_STORM: frame = t % 120 - 114 < 0 ? 1 : 2; break;
    case EYE_HURTDMG: case EYE_TIPSY: case EYE_POISON:
      frame = t % 120 - 100 < 0 ? 1 : 2; break;
    case EYE_BED: {
      const base = this.hp <= this.maxHp * 0.25 ? 1 : 0;    // :60
      this.eyeTimeInState = this.timeSleeping;               // :61 覆写计时
      frame = this.timeSleeping >= 120 ? 2 : this.timeSleeping >= 60 ? 1 : base;
      break;
    }
    case EYE_BLIND: frame = 2; break;
    case EYE_HURT: frame = 2; break;
  }
  this.eyeFrame = frame;
  this.eyeTimeInState++;   // :36
}
```

Wait careful — InBed case ORDER in vanilla: `(_timeInState >= 60) ? ((_timeInState < 120) ? Half : Closed) : eyeFrame` where _timeInState was overwritten = timeSleeping. So: ts>=120→2; 60..119→1; <60→base. ✓ my version: >=120?2 : >=60?1 : base. ✓

Note the subtle: in NormalBlinking `n >= 4 → Half(1)`. t%240-234: n∈{-234..5}. n>=4 → 1; n in [2,3] → 2; n in [0,1] → 1; n<0 → 0. ✓

private updateDeathParts(game: GameHooks): void {
  const segs = [this.deadParts.head, this.deadParts.body, this.deadParts.legs];
  for (const s of segs) {
    s.px += s.vx; s.py += s.vy;
    s.rot += s.vx * 0.1;
    s.vy += 0.1;
    s.vx *= 0.99;
  }
  // Ghost() :6435-6450 幽灵帧（ghost 态才推进）
  if (this.isGhostPhase(game.world)) {
    if (++this.ghostFrameCounter >= 8) {
      this.ghostFrameCounter = 0;
      this.ghostFrame = (this.ghostFrame + 1) % 4;
    }
  }
}
```

```ts
/** 死亡散飞渲染（LegacyPlayerRenderer.cs:479-482 dead 不跳绘制；三段头/身/腿各带
 *  headPosition/bodyPosition/legPosition 偏移 + headRotation/bodyRotation/legRotation
 *  绕 headVect(20,22.4)/bodyVect(20,28)/legVect(20,42)（PlayerDrawSet.cs:1717-1719）；
 *  alpha = GetImmuneAlpha :53253 (255−immuneAlpha)/255，immuneAlpha>125 → 全透明 */
private drawDeadPlayerParts(p: Player, world: World): void {
  const ctx = this.ctx;
  // 硬核幽灵（Player.cs:16894 difficulty==2 respawn 到点 ghost=true；Ghost() :6334-6452）
  if (p.isGhostPhase(world)) { this.drawGhostSprite(p); return; }
  if (p.immuneAlpha > 125) return;               // GetImmuneAlpha :53261-53264 全透明档
  const alpha = (255 - p.immuneAlpha) / 255;
  const a = p.appearance;
  if (!a) {
    // 兜底（登记偏离）：无纸娃娃数据 → 整体单帧带 headRotation 翻滚
    ...
  }
  let dollEquip = dollEquipFromInv(p.inv, this.atlas);
  if (p.buffs.has(BuffType.Werewolf)) dollEquip = { head: 38, body: 21, legs: 20 };
  const full = compositePaperDoll(a, dollEquip);
  if (!full) return;
  const row = this.playerBodyRow(p, null, 0, null);   // 死亡帧冻结（UpdateDead 不跑 PlayerFrame）
  const fFull = dollFrame(full, row);
  const pad = this.spriteBottomPad(fFull, `doll:${row}`);
  const parts: Array<['head'|'body'|'legs', number]> = [['legs', 42], ['body', 28], ['head', 22.4]];  // 层序 腿→身→头
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha *= alpha;
  ctx.translate(p.cx - p.facing * 2.5 + p.netOffX, p.y + p.h + p.stepRenderY + p.netOffY);
  ctx.scale(p.facing, 1);
  for (const [part, pivotY] of parts) {
    const doll = compositePaperDoll(a, dollEquip, { part });
    if (!doll) continue;
    const seg = p.deadParts[part];
    const f = dollFrame(doll, row);
    const pivotLocalY = -FRAME_H + pad + pivotY;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(seg.px, seg.py + pivotLocalY);
    ctx.rotate(seg.rot);
    ctx.translate(0, -pivotLocalY);
    ctx.drawImage(f.img, f.sx, f.sy, f.sw, f.sh, -FRAME_W / 2, -FRAME_H + pad, FRAME_W, FRAME_H);
    if (part === 'head') {
      // 眼睑同层（head 层组）——眼帧冻结于死亡值
      this.drawEyelid(ctx, p, row, pad);  // hmm — inside rotation transform? 
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.restore();
}
```

Ghost draw:

```ts
/** Ghost.png 幽灵（LegacyPlayerRenderer DrawGhost :539-553）：34×176 四帧竖排，
 *  帧 = ghostFrame；色 = mouseTextColor/2+100 脉动灰（Main.cs:18066-18075 190↔255
 *  三角波，周期 130t——以 miscCounter 为钟）；锚 = position+(w/2,h/2) 即精灵中心；
 *  GetImmuneAlpha ×（Ghost() :6340 已清 immuneAlpha=0 → 恒 alpha 1）；
 *  velocity=0 → 三条拖影(:273-281)与本体同位，只画本体 */
private drawGhostSprite(p: Player): void {
  const tex = this.atlas ? this.atlas.ensureVImage('vanilla/Ghost.png') : null;
  if (!tex || tex.width === 0) return;
  const fh = Math.floor(tex.height / 4);
  const phase = p.miscCounter % 130;
  const tri = Math.min(phase, 130 - phase);          // 0..65
  const v = 190 + tri;                                // mouseTextColor 190..255
  const g = Math.floor(v / 2) + 100;                  // 195..227
  const ctx = this.ctx;
  ctx.save();
  ctx.globalAlpha = g / 255;
  const cx = p.x + tex.width / 2, cy = p.y + fh / 2;
  ctx.translate(cx, cy);
  if (p.facing === -1) ctx.scale(-1, 1);
  ctx.drawImage(tex, 0, p.ghostFrame * fh, tex.width, fh, -tex.width / 2, -fh / 2, tex.width, fh);
  ctx.restore();
}
```

Hmm — wait: tri formula: phase 0..129: min(phase, 130-phase) → phase=65 → 65; phase=0→0; phase=129→1. Period 130, peak 65. ✓ 190+0..65 = 190..255.

this.deathHitDir : this.deathHitDir;\n    for (const seg of [this.deadParts.head, this.deadParts.body, this.deadParts.legs]) {\n      seg.px = 0; seg.py = 0; seg.rot = 0;\n      seg.vy = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 30) - 40) * 0.1;        // Next(-40,-10)×0.1\n      seg.vx = (Math.floor(Math.random() * 41) - 20) * 0.1 + 2 * hitDir;  // Next(-20,21)×0.1 + 2×hitDir\n    }\n    // :38294-38298 stoned → 三段 Position 归零\n    if (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Stoned)) {\n      for (const seg of [this.deadParts.head, this.deadParts.body, this.deadParts.legs]) {\n        seg.px = 0; seg.py = 0;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** UpdateDead（Player.cs:16878-16888）：三段 pos += vel / rot += vel.X×0.1（翻滚）/\n   *  vel.Y += 0.1（重力）/ vel.X ×= 0.99（水平阻尼）。immuneAlpha 的 +2 钳 255 由\n   *  Game 死亡分支执行（同一段逻辑，不重复）。ghost 态帧推进（Ghost() :6444-6450） */\n  private updateDeathParts(game: GameHooks): void {\n    for (const seg of [this.deadParts.head, this.deadParts.body, this.deadParts.legs]) {\n      seg.px += seg.vx;\n      seg.py += seg.vy;\n      seg.rot += seg.vx * 0.1;\n      seg.vy += 0.1;\n      seg.vx *= 0.99;\n    }\n    if (this.isGhostPhase(game.world)) {\n      if (++this.ghostFrameCounter >= 8) {\n        this.ghostFrameCounter = 0;\n        this.ghostFrame = (this.ghostFrame + 1) % 4;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** 硬核幽灵态（Player.cs:16894-16901 difficulty==2 → respawn 计时到点 ghost=true；\n   *  本仓 Game.respawnPlayer 对 hardcoreDead 拒绝重生 → 计时越过阈值即幽灵相。\n   *  boss 存活 +600 延迟未复刻（本侧不可见 boss 列表，登记偏离——仅幽灵出现时刻） */\n  isGhostPhase(world: import('../world/World').World): boolean {\n    return this.dead && this.appearance?.difficulty === 2\n      && this.respawnTimer > 600 * (mainExpertMode(world) ? 1.5 : 1);\n  }\n\n  // ============ 眼睑状态机（PlayerEyeHelper.cs 全文 1:1） ============\n\n  /** PlayerEyeHelper.Update（:32-37）：SetStateByPlayerInfo → UpdateEyeFrameToShow →\n   *  _timeInState++。逐 tick 由 fixedUpdate 驱动（原版 Player.Update :25812 调用点） */\n  private updateEyeState(game: GameHooks): void {\n    // SetStateByPlayerInfo（:75-122）——优先级：致盲 > 受击锁存 > 床 > 中度伤 > 醉 >\n    // 毒 > 风暴眯眼 > 正常眨眼\n    if (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Blackout) || this.buffs.has(BuffType.Darkness)) {\n      this.switchEyeState(EYE_BLIND);        // blackout/blind（:77-80）\n    } else if (this.eyeState === EYE_HURT && this.eyeTimeInState < 20) {\n      // 受击档锁存 20t（:83-86——期间更高优先级判定也让位）\n    } else if (this.sleeping) {\n      this.switchEyeState(EYE_BED, this.itemAnimTicks > 0);   // :87-91 reset=itemAnimation>0\n    } else if (this.hp <= this.maxHp * 0.25) {\n      this.switchEyeState(EYE_HURTDMG);      // :93-96 中度伤 = statLife ≤ statLifeMax2×0.25（:133-136）\n    } else if (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Tipsy)) {\n      this.switchEyeState(EYE_TIPSY);        // :98-101\n    } else if (this.buffs.has(BuffType.Poisoned) || this.buffs.has(BuffType.Venom)) {\n      this.switchEyeState(EYE_POISON);       // :103-106（starving 本仓无此 buff——缺省不触发）\n    } else {\n      // :108-120 风暴眯眼 = ZoneSandstorm || (ZoneSnow && IsItRaining)；behindBackWall\n      // 抑制位本仓无追踪（恒 false，登记偏离）\n      const storm = !!game.scene?.zoneSandstorm\n        || (!!game.scene?.zoneSnow && !!game.world.weather.raining);\n      this.switchEyeState(storm ? EYE_STORM : EYE_BLINK);\n    }\n    // UpdateEyeFrameToShow（:39-73）——输出 0 睁 / 1 半闭 / 2 全闭\n    const t = this.eyeTimeInState;\n    let frame: number;\n    switch (this.eyeState) {\n      case EYE_BLINK: {\n        // :46-47 正常眨眼：240t 周期末 6t——半闭 2t→闭 2t→半闭 2t\n        const n = t % 240 - 234;\n        frame = n >= 4 ? 1 : n < 2 ? (n >= 0 ? 1 : 0) : 2;\n        break;\n      }\n      case EYE_STORM:\n        frame = t % 120 - 114 < 0 ? 1 : 2;   // :51 眯眼 + 每 120t 末 6t 全闭\n        break;\n      case EYE_HURTDMG:\n      case EYE_TIPSY:\n      case EYE_POISON:\n        frame = t % 120 - 100 < 0 ? 1 : 2;   // :56 耷拉眼 + 每 120t 末 20t 全闭\n        break;\n      case EYE_BED: {\n        // :58-63 计时被 timeSleeping 覆写；<60 中度伤半闭/否则睁，60-119 半闭，≥120 全闭\n        this.eyeTimeInState = this.timeSleeping;\n        const base = this.hp <= this.maxHp * 0.25 ? 1 : 0;\n        frame = this.timeSleeping >= 120 ? 2 : this.timeSleeping >= 60 ? 1 : base;\n        break;\n      }\n      case EYE_BLIND:\n      case EYE_HURT:\n      default:\n        frame = 2;                            // :65-70 致盲/受击恒全闭\n        break;\n    }\n    this.eyeFrame = frame;\n    this.eyeTimeInState++;                    // :36（EYE_BED 已被覆写为 timeSleeping）\n  }\n\n  /** SwitchToState（:124-131）：换态清零计时（同态不重置，除显式 reset） */\n  private switchEyeState(state: number, reset = false): void {\n    if (this.eyeState !== state || reset) {\n      this.eyeState = state;\n      this.eyeTimeInState = 0;\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** BlinkBecausePlayerGotHurt（:138-141）：受击全闭 20t（Hurt :37916 调用） */\n  eyeBlinkHurt(): void {\n    this.switchEyeState(EYE_HURT, true);\n  }\n}\n\n/** PlayerEyeHelper.EyeState 枚举序（PlayerEyeHelper.cs:12-22） */\nconst EYE_BLINK = 0;    // NormalBlinking\nconst EYE_STORM = 1;    // InStorm\nconst EYE_BED = 2;      // InBed\nconst EYE_HURT = 3;     // JustTookDamage\nconst EYE_HURTDMG = 4;  // IsModeratelyDamaged\nconst EYE_BLIND = 5;    // IsBlind\nconst EYE_TIPSY = 6;    // IsTipsy\nconst EYE_POISON = 7;   // IsPoisoned"
}

dur: number; item: number; useStyle?: number; aim?: number; noGraphic?: boolean } | null, flicker: FlickerClock | null = null) {\n    const ctx = this.ctx;\n    if (p.dead) { this.drawDeadPlayerParts(p, world); 'http://localhost:5201') + '/?play=small', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });\nawait page.waitForFunction(() => { const g = window.__swGame; return g && g.player; }, { timeout: 110000 });\nawait new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1500));\n\n// ---- ② 眨眼（先测——不弄脏玩家状态） ----\nconst eye = await page.evaluate(async () => {\n  const g = window.__swGame, p = g.player;\n  const seen = new Set();\n  const seq = [];   // [t, eyeFrame]\n  const t0 = performance.now();\n  while (performance.now() - t0 < 4600) {   // > 240t（60Hz）\n    seen.add(p.eyeFrame);\n    seq.push([Math.round(performance.now() - t0), p.eyeFrame]);\n    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 60));\n  }\n  // 受击全闭（BlinkBecausePlayerGotHurt :138——damage 落伤后立即 2，锁存 20t）\n  p.eyeBlinkHurt();\n  const hurtA = p.eyeFrame;\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 150));   // 20t+ 后回落（无其他触发时回正常眨眼档）\n  const hurtAfter = p.eyeFrame;\n  return { frames: [...seen].sort(), blinkSamples: seq.filter(s => s[1] !== 0).length, hurtA, hurtAfter };\n});\n\n// ---- 眼睑像素实证：闭眼时眼区瞳孔暗像素被皮肤色眼睑覆盖 ----\nconst lid = await page.evaluate(async () => {\n  const g = window.__swGame, p = g.player;\n  const cv = g.renderer.canvas, cam = g.camera;\n  const darkInHead = () => {\n    const top = p.y + p.h - 56 + 2;    // 纸娃娃帧顶（脚底锚 −56+pad2）\n    const sx = Math.round((p.cx - p.facing * 2.5 - cam.x) * cam.zoom + cv.width / 2);\n    const sy = Math.round((top - cam.y) * cam.zoom + cv.height / 2);\n    const w = Math.round(26 * cam.zoom), h = Math.round(24 * cam.zoom);\n    const img = g.renderer.ctx.getImageData(Math.max(0, sx - w / 2), Math.max(0, sy), w, h).data;\n    let dark = 0, skin = 0;\n    for (let i = 0; i < img.length; i += 4) {\n      if (img[i + 3] > 100 && img[i] < 165 && img[i + 1] < 150) dark++;        // 瞳/眼线暗像素\n      else if (img[i + 3] > 100 && img[i] > 200 && img[i + 1] > 90 && img[i + 1] < 175) skin++; // 皮肤/衣亮像素\n    }\n    return { dark, skin };\n  };\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));\n  const open = darkInHead();\n  p.eyeBlinkHurt();\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 120));\n  const closed = darkInHead();\n  return { open, closed, covered: closed.dark < open.dark };\n});\n\n// ---- ① 死亡散飞 ----\nconst death = await page.evaluate(async () => {\n  const g = window.__swGame, p = g.player;\n  const cv = g.renderer.canvas, cam = g.camera;\n  // 死亡位周边皮肤/衣色像素采样（排除红色粉碎粒子：g<95 全是血尘色带）\n  const bodyPx = () => {\n    const sx = Math.round((p.x + p.w / 2 - cam.x) * cam.zoom + cv.width / 2);\n    const sy = Math.round((p.y + p.h / 2 - cam.y) * cam.zoom + cv.height / 2);\n    const w = Math.round(140 * cam.zoom), h = Math.round(160 * cam.zoom);\n    const img = g.renderer.ctx.getImageData(Math.max(0, sx - w / 2), Math.max(0, sy - h / 2), w, h).data;\n    let body = 0, blood = 0;\n    for (let i = 0; i < img.length; i += 4) {\n      if (img[i + 3] > 80 && img[i] > 180 && img[i + 1] > 95 && img[i + 2] > 55) body++;\n      else if (img[i + 3] > 80 && img[i] > 140 && img[i] > img[i + 1] * 2 && img[i] > img[i + 2] * 2) blood++;\n    }\n    return { body, blood };\n  };\n  const px0 = bodyPx();   // 死前存活基线（完整身体）\n  const before = { dead: p.dead, hp: p.hp, parts: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p.deadParts)) };\n  p.damage(99999, p.cx + 20, p.y);   // 右侧来刀 → deathHitDir = -1\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 60));\n  const t0 = {\n    dead: p.dead, hp: p.hp,\n    seg: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p.deadParts)),\n    px: bodyPx(), immuneAlpha: p.immuneAlpha, respawn: p.respawnTimer,\n  };\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 450));\n  const tMid = { seg: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p.deadParts)), px: bodyPx(), immuneAlpha: p.immuneAlpha };\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1750));   // 合计 ~2.26s\n  const tFade = { px: bodyPx(), immuneAlpha: p.immuneAlpha, respawn: p.respawnTimer };\n  const vy0 = [before.parts.head.vy, before.parts.body.vy, before.parts.legs.vy];\n  return {\n    before, t0, tMid, tFade, px0,\n    vyRangeOk: t0.seg.head.vy >= -4.01 && t0.seg.head.vy <= -1.09 && t0.seg.legs.vy < 0,\n    xHitDir: t0.seg.head.vx < 0,   // deathHitDir −1 → X = rand*0.1 − 2（大概率负向）\n    scattered: Math.abs(tMid.seg.head.px - tMid.seg.legs.px) + Math.abs(tMid.seg.head.py - tMid.seg.legs.py) > 4,\n    rolled: tMid.seg.head.rot !== 0 || tMid.seg.body.rot !== 0,\n    vy0,\n  };\n});\n\n// ---- ③ 硬核幽灵 ----\nconst ghost = await page.evaluate(async () => {\n  const g = window.__swGame, p = g.player;\n  // 软核下 10s 自动重生复活——重杀一次并钉死硬核难度\n  p.appearance = { ...p.appearance, difficulty: 2 };\n  p.damage(99999, p.cx, p.cy);\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));\n  const deadX = p.x, deadY = p.y;\n  const phaseEarly = p.isGhostPhase(g.world);\n  // 等待 respawn 阈值（600t=10s；世界经典档非专家）+ 一点余量\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 10900));\n  const cv = g.renderer.canvas, cam = g.camera;\n  const sx = Math.round((p.x + 17 - cam.x) * cam.zoom + cv.width / 2);\n  const sy = Math.round((p.y + 22 - cam.y) * cam.zoom + cv.height / 2);\n  const img = g.renderer.ctx.getImageData(Math.max(0, sx - 20), Math.max(0, sy - 26), 40, 52).data;\n  let bright = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < img.length; i += 4) if (img[i + 3] > 100) bright++;\n  return {\n    phaseEarly, ghostPhase: p.isGhostPhase(g.world), dead: p.dead,\n    respawn: p.respawnTimer, ghostFrame: p.ghostFrame, at: [deadX, deadY],\n    px: bright,\n  };\n});\n\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({ eye, lid, death, ghost }, null, 1));\nawait page.screenshot({ path: '/tmp/deathvis.png' });\nawait browser.close();\n"
}

{

 "old_string": "// ---- ③ 硬核幽灵 ----\nconst ghost = await page.evaluate(async () => {\n  const g = window.__swGame, p = g.player;\n  // 软核下 10s 自动重生复活——重杀一次并钉死硬核难度\n  p.appearance = { ...p.appearance, difficulty: 2 };\n  p.damage(99999, p.cx, p.cy);\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));\n  const deadX = p.x, deadY = p.y;\n  const phaseEarly = p.isGhostPhase(g.world);\n  // 等待 respawn 阈值（600t=10s；世界经典档非专家）+ 一点余量\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 10900));\n  const cv = g.renderer.canvas, cam = g.camera;\n  const sx = Math.round((p.x + 17 - cam.x) * cam.zoom + cv.width / 2);\n  const sy = Math.round((p.y + 22 - cam.y) * cam.zoom + cv.height / 2);\n  const img = g.renderer.ctx.getImageData(Math.max(0, sx - 20), Math.max(0, sy - 26), 40, 52).data;\n  let bright = 0;\n  for (let i = 0; i < img.length; i += 4) if (img[i + 3] > 100) bright++;\n  return {\n    phaseEarly, ghostPhase: p.isGhostPhase(g.world), dead: p.dead,\n    respawn: p.respawnTimer, ghostFrame: p.ghostFrame, at: [deadX, deadY],\n    px: bright,\n  };\n});",
 "new_string": "// ---- ③ 硬核幽灵 ----\nconst ghost = await page.evaluate(async () => {\n  const g = window.__swGame, p = g.player;\n  // 前一段死亡在软核下 ~10s 自动重生——先等复活（软核阈值 600t）\n  const t0 = performance.now();\n  while (p.dead && performance.now() - t0 < 12500) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 200));\n  if (p.dead) return { skipped: 'player never respawned', respawn: p.respawnTimer };\n  // 钉死硬核难度再杀（Game 侧会标 hardcoreDead → respawnPlayer 永拒 → 计时越 600 即幽灵）\n  p.appearance = { ...p.appearance, difficulty: 2 };\n  p.hp = Math.min(p.hp, 100);\n  p.damage(99999, p.cx, p.cy);\n  const t1 = performance.now();\n  while (!p.dead && performance.now() - t1 < 2000) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 60));\n  if (!p.dead) return { skipped: 'kill failed', hp: p.hp };\n  const deadX = p.x, deadY = p.y;\n  const phaseEarly = p.isGhostPhase(g.world);\n  // 等 respawn 阈值（经典档 600t=10s；专家 900t）+余量\n  const t2 = performance.now();\n  while (!p.isGhostPhase(g.world) && performance.now() - t2 < 20500) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 200));\n  const ghostPhase = p.isGhostPhase(g.world);\n  const cv = g.renderer.canvas, cam = g.camera;\n  const ghostPx = () => {\n    const sx = Math.round((p.x + 17 - cam.x) * cam.zoom + cv.width / 2);\n    const sy = Math.round((p.y + 22 - cam.y) * cam.zoom + cv.height / 2);\n    const img = g.renderer.ctx.getImageData(Math.max(0, sx - 17), Math.max(0, sy - 22), 34, 44).data;\n    let n = 0;\n    for (let i = 0; i < img.length; i += 4) if (img[i + 3] > 100 && Math.abs(img[i] - img[i + 1]) < 24 && Math.abs(img[i + 1] - img[i + 2]) < 24 && img[i] > 140) n++;\n    return n;\n  };\n  const px = ghostPx();\n  const fA = p.ghostFrame;\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 700));   // 8t/帧 → 至少推进 5 帧\n  const fB = p.ghostFrame;\n  return {\n    phaseEarly, ghostPhase, dead: p.dead, hardcoreDead: !!p.appearance.hardcoreDead,\n    respawn: p.respawnTimer, frameA: fA, frameB: fB, at: [deadX, deadY], px,\n  };\n});"
}

'http://localhost:5201') + '/?play=small', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });\nawait page.waitForFunction(() => { const g = window.__swGame; return g && g.player; }, { timeout: 110000 });\nawait new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1500));\nconst out = await page.evaluate(async () => {\n  const g = window.__swGame, p = g.player;\n  const cv = g.renderer.canvas, cam = g.camera;\n  // 全画布扫描皮肤色簇（默认 skinColor 255,125,90 ±28）\n  const img = g.renderer.ctx.getImageData(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height).data;\n  let minX = 1e9, minY = 1e9, maxX = -1, maxY = -1, n = 0;\n  for (let y = 0; y < cv.height; y += 2) {\n    for (let x = 0; x < cv.width; x += 2) {\n      const i = (y * cv.width + x) * 4;\n      if (img[i + 3] > 100 && Math.abs(img[i] - 255) < 30 && Math.abs(img[i + 1] - 125) < 32 && Math.abs(img[i + 2] - 90) < 32) {\n        n++; if (x < minX) minX = x; if (x > maxX) maxX = x; if (y < minY) minY = y; if (y > maxY) maxY = y;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  const expX = Math.round((p.cx - cam.x) * cam.zoom + cv.width / 2);\n  const expFootY = Math.round((p.y + p.h - cam.y) * cam.zoom + cv.height / 2);\n  return {\n    found: n, bbox: [minX, minY, maxX, maxY], center: [(minX + maxX) / 2, (minY + maxY) / 2],\n    expected: { playerX: expX, footY: expFootY },\n    cam: { x: cam.x, y: cam.y, zoom: cam.zoom }, cv: [cv.width, cv.height],\n    p: { x: p.x, y: p.y, w: p.w, h: p.h, cx: p.cx, facing: p.facing },\n  };\n});\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 1));\nawait browser.close();\n"
}

'http://localhost:5201') + '/?play=small', { waitUntil: 'domcontentloaded' });\nawait page.waitForFunction(() => { const g = window.__swGame; return g && g.player; }, { timeout: 110000 });\nawait new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1500));\nconst out = await page.evaluate(async () => {\n  const g = window.__swGame, p = g.player;\n  const cv = g.renderer.canvas, cam = g.camera;\n  const a = p.appearance;\n  if (!a) return { noAppearance: true };\n  // 用实际皮肤色全画布找簇\n  const img = g.renderer.ctx.getImageData(0, 0, cv.width, cv.height).data;\n  const near = (v, t, tol) => Math.abs(v - t) <= tol;\n  let minX = 1e9, minY = 1e9, maxX = -1, maxY = -1, n = 0;\n  for (let y = 0; y < cv.height; y += 2) {\n    for (let x = 0; x < cv.width; x += 2) {\n      const i = (y * cv.width + x) * 4;\n      if (img[i + 3] > 100 && near(img[i], a.skinColor.r, 34) && near(img[i + 1], a.skinColor.g, 36) && near(img[i + 2], a.skinColor.b, 34)) {\n        n++; if (x < minX) minX = x; if (x > maxX) maxX = x; if (y < minY) minY = y; if (y > maxY) maxY = y;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  const expX = Math.round((p.cx - cam.x) * cam.zoom + cv.width / 2);\n  const expFootY = Math.round((p.y + p.h - cam.y) * cam.zoom + cv.height / 2);\n  const expTopY = Math.round((p.y + p.h - 56 + 2 - cam.y) * cam.zoom + cv.height / 2);\n  // 眼区暗像素（瞳/眉/发际暗色，排除皮肤色）\n  const eyeBox = () => {\n    const w = Math.round(30 * cam.zoom), h = Math.round(22 * cam.zoom);\n    const reg = g.renderer.ctx.getImageData(Math.max(0, expX - w / 2), Math.max(0, expTopY + Math.round(4 * cam.zoom)), w, h).data;\n    let dark = 0, tot = 0;\n    for (let i = 0; i < reg.length; i += 4) {\n      if (reg[i + 3] > 100) { tot++; if (reg[i] < 165 && reg[i + 1] < 150) dark++; }\n    }\n    return { dark, tot };\n  };\n  const open = eyeBox();\n  p.eyeBlinkHurt();\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 130));\n  const closed = eyeBox();\n  return {\n    skin: a.skinColor, hair: a.hairColor, eye: a.eyeColor, skinVariant: a.skinVariant,\n    cluster: { n, bbox: [minX, minY, maxX, maxY] },\n    expected: { x: expX, footY: expFootY, topY: expTopY },\n    zoom: cam.zoom, open, closed,\n  };\n});\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 1));\nawait browser.close();\n"

i < img.length; i += 4) {\n      if (img[i + 3] < 100) continue;\n      if (img[i] < 60 && img[i + 1] > 200 && img[i + 2] < 80) neonN++;\n      else if (img[i] > 200 && img[i + 1] > 200 && img[i + 2] > 120 && img[i + 2] < 220) pantsN++;\n    }\n    return { neon: neonN, pants: pantsN };\n  };\n  const pxAlive = boxPx();\n  p.damage(99999, p.cx + 20, p.y);             // 右侧来刀 → deathHitDir = −1\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 90));\n  const t0 = {\n    dead: p.dead, hp: p.hp, hd: p.deathHitDir, seg: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p.deadParts)),\n    px: boxPx(), immuneAlpha: p.immuneAlpha,\n  };\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 430));\n  const tMid = { seg: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p.deadParts)), px: boxPx(), immuneAlpha: p.immuneAlpha };\n  const deathShot = 'mid';   // 标记（外层截图）\n  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 800));   // 合计 ~1.32s：immuneAlpha>125 全隐\n  const tHide = { px: boxPx(), immuneAlpha: p.immuneAlpha };\n  const death = {\n    pxAlive, t0, tMid, tHide, deathShot,\n    vyRangeOk: t0.seg.head.vy >= -4.01 && t0.seg.head.vy <= -1.09 && t0.seg.legs.vy < 0 && t0.seg.body.vy < 0,\n    xHitDir: t0.seg.head.vx <= 0 && t0.seg.legs.vx <= 0,   // deathHitDir−1 → X∈[−4,0]\n    scattered: Math.abs(tMid.seg.head.px - tMid.seg.legs.px) + Math.abs(tMid.seg.head.py - tMid.seg.legs.py) > 4,\n    rolled: Math.abs(tMid.seg.head.rot) > 0.5 || Math.abs(tMid.seg.body.rot) > 0.5,\n    partsVisible: t0.px.neon + t0.px.pants - tHide.px.neon - tHide.px.pants,\n  };\n\n  // ---- ③ 硬核幽灵 ----\n  const tw = performance.now();\n  while (p.dead && performance.now() - tw < 12500) await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 200));                  // 阈值前（同位无幽灵）地形灰基线\n      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 700));\n      const withGhost = grayPx();\n      const fA = p.ghostFrame;\n      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 700));   // 8t/帧 → ≥5 帧推进\n      ghost = {\n        phaseEarly, ghostPhase: p.isGhostPhase(g.world), hardcoreDead: !!p.appearance.hardcoreDead,\n        respawn: p.respawnTimer, frameA: fA, frameB: p.ghostFrame,\n        baseGray: base, ghostGray: withGhost, ghostPx: withGhost - base,\n      };\n    } else {\n      ghost = { skipped: 'kill failed', hp: p.hp };\n    }\n  }\n  return { eye, death, ghost };\n});\n\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(out, null, 1));\nawait page.screenshot({ path: '/tmp/deathvis.png' });\nawait browser.close();\n"

```
 *  用法:im.onload 里先照旧 set(Image),再调 upgradeToBitmap(im, b => map.set(k, b))
 *  ——消费方每帧重查,下一帧起拿到的就是自持解码像素;契约零变化 */
export function upgradeToBitmap(img: HTMLImageElement, onReady: (b: ImageBitmap) => void, onFail?: () => void): void {
  if (!SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP) return;
  createImageBitmap(img).then(onReady, () => onFail?.());  // 失败通知调用方(缺省静默)
}
/** 通用 bitmap-only 加载器(2026-08-17 修 trace⑨ 收尾两个真 bug):
 *  ①在飞守卫——此前未就绪期间每帧重 new Image = 请求风暴(雨/尘每粒子每帧)
 *  ②失败回退存 Image——此前失败静默 = 纹理永久缺(用户报"贴图丢失") */
const _bmpOnlyPending = new Set<string>();
export function loadBitmapOnly(
  file: string, has: () => boolean, store: (x: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement) => void,
): void {
  if (has() || _bmpOnlyPending.has(file)) return;
  _bmpOnlyPending.add(file);
  const im = new Image();
  im.onload = () => {
    const land = (x: ImageBitmap | HTMLImageElement) => { store(x); _bmpOnlyPending.delete(file); };
    if (SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP) createImageBitmap(im).then(land, () => land(im));  // 失败回退 Image,永不缺图
    else land(im);
  };
  im.onerror = () => _bmpOnlyPending.delete(file);
  im.src = `sprites/${file}`;
}

export const USE_BITMAP = SpriteAtlas.USE_BITMAP;

 "new_string": "      // 幽灵像素实证：夜幕光照会把灰幽灵压暗（原版同样乘 Lighting.GetColor）——\n      // 用「幽灵矩形 vs 左移同尺寸矩形」的平均亮度差（幽灵叠加灰 = 增亮）\n      const lum = (wx, wy) => {\n        const [sx, sy] = scr(wx, wy);\n        const img = g.renderer.ctx.getImageData(Math.max(0, sx - 21), Math.max(0, sy - 28), 42, 56).data;\n        let s = 0, n = 0;\n        for (let i = 0; i < img.length; i += 4) { if (img[i + 3] > 100) { s += (img[i] + img[i + 1] + img[i + 2]) / 3; n++; } }\n        return n ? s / n : 0;\n      };"
}
